bind_go__passToJS_.go => 	if e1 == nil { 
bind_go__passToJS_.go => 		return y1
bind_go__passToJS_.go => 	} 
bind_go__passToJS_.go => 	y2, e2 := strconv.Atoi(  "0"+strings.TrimSpace(x)  ) 
bind_go__passToJS_.go => 	if e2 == nil {
bind_go__passToJS_.go => 		return y2
bind_go__passToJS_.go => 	} else {
bind_go__passToJS_.go => 		fmt.Println("error in getInt(",x,") ", e2) 
bind_go__passToJS_.go => 	}
bind_go__passToJS_.go => 	return 0

bind_go_word.go => func getRowIndicesFromIxFreqWord(ixWord int, maxNumRow int) []int {
bind_go_word.go => 
bind_go_word.go => 		var xWordF     = uniqueWordByFreq[ixWord]   	
bind_go_word.go => 
bind_go_word.go => 		var ixFromList = xWordF.uIxWordFreq                     // index of this word in the wordSliceFreq	( una word per ogni row ) 
bind_go_word.go => 		var ixToList   = ixFromList + xWordF.uTotRow;
bind_go_word.go => 		var maxTo1     = ixFromList + maxNumRow; 		
bind_go_word.go => 		
bind_go_word.go => 		if ixToList > maxTo1        { ixToList = maxTo1; }
bind_go_word.go => 		if ixToList > numberOfWords { ixToList = numberOfWords; }
bind_go_word.go => 
bind_go_word.go => 		listIxRR := make([]int,0, (ixToList - ixFromList) )
bind_go_word.go => 		
bind_go_word.go => 		for ix4:=ixFromList; ix4 < ixToList; ix4++ {
bind_go_word.go => 			wS1 := wordSliceFreq[ix4] 
bind_go_word.go => 			//  .wIxRow           indice del row che contiene la parola 	
bind_go_word.go => 			//  .wSwSelRowR	      1 or 2: 1 SEL_EXTR_ROW, 2 SEL_NO_EXTR_ROW  ( serve per dare la precedenza alle righe che appartengono al brano selezionato    
bind_go_word.go => 			listIxRR = append( listIxRR, wS1.wSwSelRowR * 100000 +  wS1.wIxRow)  	
bind_go_word.go => 		} 	
bind_go_word.go => 		
bind_go_word.go => 		sort.Ints(listIxRR) 
bind_go_word.go => 		
bind_go_word.go => 		return listIxRR 
bind_go_word.go => 		
bind_go_word.go => } // end of getRowIndicesFromIxFreqWord

bind_go_word_to_row.go => func add_wordCombinations( wordL1 []string, wordL2 []string )  []string {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	wor3List:= []string{} 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	for _, wor1 := range wordL1 {	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if wor1 == "" {continue}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		for _, wor2 := range wordL2 {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if wor2 == "" {continue}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			wor3List = append(wor3List, wor1 + wor2  ) ;
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			wor3List = append(wor3List, wor2 + wor1  ) ;
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			wor3List = append(wor3List, wor1 + "s" + wor2  ) ;
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			wor3List = append(wor3List, wor2 + "s" + wor1  ) ;			
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		}	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	}	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	newWordList:= []string{}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	for _, wor1 := range wor3List {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		word1 := checkTheWord( wor1 ) ;
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if word1 == "" { continue }					
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		wordCod:= seqCode( word1 )	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		_, ixT:= lookForWordInUniqueAlpha( wordCod)	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if (ixT >= 0) { newWordList = append(newWordList, strings.ToLower(word1))	}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	}	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	return newWordList
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => } // end of add_wordCombinations

bind_go_word_to_row.go => func getRowIndexFromWordIndex( wordA00 []string, swComb bool, maxNumRow int) ( string, string, []int) {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	listRowIndices := make([]int,0, maxNumRow)
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	listIxRR := make([]int,0, maxNumRow)
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	var listWords, listLemma string
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	//-----------------
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	for _, wor1 := range wordA00 {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		word1 := checkTheWord( wor1 ) ;
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if word1 == "" { continue }					
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		wordCod:= seqCode( word1 )	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		ixF, ixT:= lookForWordInUniqueAlpha( wordCod)	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if (ixT < 0) { 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if swComb == false {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 				listWords += " " +  word1
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 				listLemma += word1 + "|||\n" 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			continue 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		ixWord:= -1 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		for ix:= ixF; ix <= ixT; ix++ {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			xWordA :=  uniqueWordByAlpha[ix] 			
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if xWordA.uWordSeq != wordCod { continue } // get only the required word (might be several entries of the same word) and then the list of lemmas of this word 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			ixWord = xWordA.uIxUnW			
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if ixWord >= numberOfUniqueWords {ixWord = numberOfUniqueWords - 1;}	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if ixWord < 0 { continue }
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			listRowIndices = getRowIndicesFromIxFreqWord(ixWord, maxNumRow)
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if len(listRowIndices) < 1 {continue} 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			listIxRR = append(listIxRR, listRowIndices...)
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		} // end for ix 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		//------------
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if ixWord < 0 { 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if swComb == false {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 				listWords += " " +  word1
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 				listLemma += word1 + "|||\n" 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			continue 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		} 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		xWordF  := uniqueWordByFreq[ixWord]   
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		listWords += " " +  xWordF.uWord2
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		//--------
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		for z:=0; z < len(xWordF.uLemmaL); z++  {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			ixL1:= xWordF.uIxLemmaL[z]
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			LeS := lemmaSlice[ixL1]
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			newL:= xWordF.uLemmaL[z]
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if newL != LeS.leLemma { continue}  // error 			
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if len(newL) > 1 { if newL[0:1] == "?" { newL = ""} }
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			newT:= LeS.leTran
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			newP:= xWordF.uPara[z]			
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if newP == "" { newP = newL}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			if z == 0 {	listLemma += xWordF.uWord2} 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			listLemma += "|" + newP + "|" + newT + "\n" 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		} // end for z	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	} // end of range wordA00		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	return listWords, listLemma, listIxRR	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => } // end of getRowIndexFromWordIndex

bind_go_word_to_row.go => func getWordList(aWordList0 string, maxNumList int) []string {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	wordA0 := regexp.MustCompile(separWord).Split(aWordList0, -1)  // split row into words 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	wordAlist := make([]string,0, 2*maxNumList) 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	for _, wor00 := range wordA0 {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if len(wordAlist) >= maxNumList { return wordAlist }
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if strings.Index(wor00,"-") < 0 { 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			wordAlist = append(wordAlist, wor00)
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			continue 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		_, wordPrefixIndexList, wordSuffixIndexList := get_word_row_list( maxNumList, wor00) 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		for _, ixWord:= range wordPrefixIndexList {  if ixWord >= 0 { wordAlist = append(wordAlist, uniqueWordByAlpha[ixWord].uWord2 )  } }
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		for _, ixWord:= range wordSuffixIndexList {  if ixWord >= 0 { wordAlist = append(wordAlist, uniqueWordByAlpha[ixWord].uWord2 )  } } 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	} 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	if len(wordAlist) <= maxNumList { return wordAlist }
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	return wordAlist[0:maxNumList]
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => } // end of getWordList

bind_go_word_to_row.go => func fun_wordListToRowList_dett( ixWord int, maxNumRow int) []int {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		var xWordF     = uniqueWordByFreq[ixWord]   
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		var ixFromList = xWordF.uIxWordFreq 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		var ixToList   = ixFromList + xWordF.uTotRow;
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		var maxTo1     = ixFromList + maxNumRow; 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if ixToList > maxTo1        { ixToList = maxTo1; }
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if ixToList > numberOfWords { ixToList = numberOfWords; }		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 				
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		/*		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		here are scanned all the rows containing the word required (with index ixWord) 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		for each line totMinRow is the number of words in the line with lower reference than the required word (ie. not studied yet)	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		*/
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		listIxRR := make([]int,0, ixToList-ixFromList)
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if (ixFromList < 1) { ixFromList=0;}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		for n1 := ixFromList; n1 < ixToList; n1++  {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			wS1 := wordSliceFreq[n1] 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			//fmt.Println("fun_wordListToRowList_dett() wS1=", wS1);
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			listIxRR = append( listIxRR, wS1.wSwSelRowR * 100000 +  wS1.wIxRow) 			
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		} 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		return listIxRR
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => } // end fun_wordListToRowList

bind_go_word_to_row.go => func bind_go_passToJs_rowList(inpBegRow int, maxNumRow int, selFrasiParole12 int, js_function1R string, js_function2W string, caller string) {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	// lista tutte le frasi richieste ( numero della prima frase, numero di frasi) 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	swLIST_WORD := (selFrasiParole12 == 2)    // 1 = list chosen rows,  2 = list words in the chosen rows 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	var ixFromList = inpBegRow 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	var outS1 string;
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	numOut:=0 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	new_rIdRow :=""
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	wordIxList:= make([]int, 0, 10000)
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	//fmt.Println("bind_go_passToJs_rowList  selFrasiParole12=",  selFrasiParole12, " len=",  len(inputTextRowSlice) ) 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	for ixRR := ixFromList; ixRR < len(inputTextRowSlice); ixRR++  {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		rline := inputTextRowSlice[ixRR]
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		rowX := cleanRow(rline.rRow1)	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		//fmt.Println("bind_go_passToJs_rowList  rline=", rline)
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if ((rowX =="") || (rowX == LAST_WORD)) { 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			continue 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		}		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		numOut++
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if (numOut > maxNumRow)  { break }
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if rline.rixGroup < 0 { 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			new_rIdRow = "- " + strconv.Itoa( rline.rixBaseGroup ) + "(" + rline.rIdRow +  " " + strconv.Itoa(ixRR) 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		} else {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			new_rIdRow = lista_gruppiSelectRow[ rline.rixGroup ].rG_group + " " + strconv.Itoa( rline.rixBaseGroup ) + "(" + rline.rIdRow +  " " + strconv.Itoa(ixRR) 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if swLIST_WORD {

bind_go_word_to_row.go => func bind_go_passToJs_rowWordList(numIdOut string, ixRR int, js_function string) {
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	//  lista di tutte le parole di una frase

bind_go_word_to_row.go =>

bind_go_word_to_row.go => 			endOfLine

bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 	}	
bind_go_word_to_row.go =>

bind_go_word_to_row.go => func cleanRow(row0 string) string{
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		row1 := strings.ReplaceAll( row0, "<br>", " "   ); 	// remove <br> because it's needed to split the lines to transmit  
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		// if the row begins with a number remove this number
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		row1 = strings.Trim(row1," \t")    // trim space and tab code
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		k:=    strings.IndexAny(row1, " \t");	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if (k <1) { return row1;}
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		numS := row1[:k]	
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		_, err := strconv.Atoi(numS)
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		if err != nil { return row1;}  
bind_go_word_to_row.go => 		return strings.Trim(row1[k+1:]," \t"); 
bind_go_word_to_row.go => }
bind_go_word_to_row.go =>

bind_go_write.go => func split_one_word_dict_row( row1 string ) (string, int, []string, []string) {
bind_go_write.go => 
bind_go_write.go => 	// eg. einem;14; ein§einem§einer	
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	lemmaLis := make( []string,0,0 )
bind_go_write.go => 	tranLis  := make( []string,0,0 )
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	if row1 == "" { return "",	-1,	lemmaLis, tranLis }                           
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	//row1:= strings.ReplaceAll( strings.ReplaceAll( row0, ....  parentesi quadre 
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	var field = strings.Split( row1, ";");     // eg. einem;  14; ein§einem§einer; uno§uno§uno
bind_go_write.go => 											   //	 field[0]; 1;               2; 3 
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	//lemmaLis = strings.Fields( strings.ReplaceAll(field[2],wSep," ") )       
bind_go_write.go => 	//tranLis  = strings.Fields( strings.ReplaceAll(field[3],wSep," ") )  
bind_go_write.go => 	lemmaLis = strings.Split( strings.Trim(field[2],wSep), wSep )  
bind_go_write.go => 	tranLis  = strings.Split( strings.Trim(field[3],wSep), wSep )  
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	//fmt.Println("  xx split_one_word_dict_row(", row1, ") file=", field, " lemmaLis=", lemmaLis, "tranLis=", tranLis )  
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	if len(lemmaLis) != len(tranLis)  {
bind_go_write.go => 		return "",	-1,	lemmaLis, tranLis    
bind_go_write.go => 	} 
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 	ix1, err := strconv.Atoi( field[1] )
bind_go_write.go => 	if err != nil { return "",	-1,	lemmaLis, tranLis }                           //error
bind_go_write.go => 	 
bind_go_write.go => 	return field[0],ix1, lemmaLis, tranLis     // eg. return einem; 14, [ein , einem,   einer], [uno , uno,     uno] 
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => } // end of	split_one_Word_Dict_row(

bind_go_write.go => func update_rowTranslation( rowDictRow [] string)  {
bind_go_write.go => 	/*
bind_go_write.go => 	rowDictRow is filled in javascript, each line: rowIndex;row Translation   
bind_go_write.go => 	*/
bind_go_write.go => 	var len1 = len(inputTextRowSlice)
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	//fmt.Println("update_rowTraslation() len(rowDictRow) =",len(rowDictRow), " = ", rowDictRow)
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	//ion() len(rowDictRow) =  [ 2 206(2_206 211|211|1. Introduzione Preistoria 2 207(2_207 212|212|La prei
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 	for z:=0; z < len(rowDictRow); z++ {  		
bind_go_write.go => 		/**
bind_go_write.go => 		2;Primo capitolo
bind_go_write.go => 		**/
bind_go_write.go => 		row1dict := rowDictRow[z] 	
bind_go_write.go => 		if row1dict == "" { continue }	
bind_go_write.go => 		rowCols	 := strings.Split(row1dict, "|")
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 		//fmt.Println("\trowCols= len=", len(rowCols), " rowcols=",    rowCols) 
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 		if len(rowCols) < 3 { 
bind_go_write.go => 			fmt.Println("update_rowTraslation() len(rowDictRow) z=", z, " row1dict=", row1dict, " len(rowCols)=", len(rowCols))
bind_go_write.go => 			continue 
bind_go_write.go => 		}
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 		idS   := rowCols[0]   
bind_go_write.go => 		ixS   := rowCols[1] 
bind_go_write.go => 			
bind_go_write.go => 		tranS := rowCols[2]
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 		//fmt.Println("\tidS=", idS, "  ixS=", ixS,  " tranS=", tranS ) 
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => 		ixRow, err := strconv.Atoi(  strings.TrimSpace( ixS) )		
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 		//fmt.Println("\tixRow=", ixRow, " err=", err)
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 		if err != nil {
bind_go_write.go => 			return 
bind_go_write.go => 		}
bind_go_write.go => 		if ixRow >= len1 { return }  // error 	
bind_go_write.go => 
bind_go_write.go => 		//fmt.Println("\tinputTextRowSlice[ixRow].rIdRow = ", inputTextRowSlice[ixRow].rIdRow )
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 		if strings.Index( idS, inputTextRowSlice[ixRow].rIdRow) < 0  {
bind_go_write.go => 			// error
bind_go_write.go => 			fmt.Println("error func update_rowTranslation () inputTextRowSlice[ixRow=", ixRow, "].rIdRow = ", inputTextRowSlice[ixRow].rIdRow,   " idS=",  idS )
bind_go_write.go => 			continue
bind_go_write.go => 		}  
bind_go_write.go => 		inputTextRowSlice[ixRow].rTran1 = strings.TrimSpace(tranS ) 
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 		//fmt.Println("update_rowTraslation() row1dict=", row1dict, " \n\t ixRow=", ixRow, " inputTextRowSlice[ixRow]=", inputTextRowSlice[ixRow] )
bind_go_write.go => 		
bind_go_write.go => 	} // end for z
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => } // end of update_rowTranslation

bind_go_write.go => func writeTextRowSlice() {		
bind_go_write.go =>

bind_go_write.go => 		lines:= make([]string, 0, 10+len( inputTextRowSlice) )

bind_go_write.go => 		}

bind_go_write.go => func bind_go_passToJs_write_WordsToLearn(js_function string)  {
bind_go_write.go =>

bind_go_write.go => 	for i:= 0; i < len(uniqueWordByFreq); i++ { 
bind_go_write.go => 		var xWordF     = uniqueWordByFreq[i]		
bind_go_write.go => 		//if ( (xWordF.uKnow_yes_ctr < 1 ) && (xWordF.uKnow_no_ctr < 1 ))  { continue }
bind_go_write.go => 		//lines = append( lines,   fmt.Sprintf( "%s|%d|%d",  xWordF.uWord2, xWordF.uKnow_yes_ctr,  xWordF.uKnow_no_ctr) ) 
bind_go_write.go => 		lines = append( lines,  xWordF.uWord2 + "|" + xWordF.uLearnedYN  ) 
bind_go_write.go => 		numOut++	
bind_go_write.go => 	}	
bind_go_write.go =>

bind_go_write.go => 	if (numOut > 0) { 	
bind_go_write.go => 		writeList( outFile, lines )
bind_go_write.go => 		//--------
bind_go_write.go => 		currentTime := time.Now()	
bind_go_write.go => 		outF1 		:= FOLDER_O_arc_TO_learn + string(os.PathSeparator)  
bind_go_write.go => 		outFile2    := outF1 + "wordsToLearn_"  + currentTime.Format("20060102150405") + ".txt"

bind_go_write.go => 	}
bind_go_write.go => 	//-----------------	
bind_go_write.go =>

bind_go_write.go => 		fmt.Println(outS1) 
bind_go_write.go => 		return 
bind_go_write.go => 	}
bind_go_write.go => 	go_exec_js_function( js_function, outS1 ); 	
bind_go_write.go => 	
bind_go_write.go => } // end of  bind_go_passToJs_write_WordsToLearn

build_word_list.go => func checkTheWord( word0 string ) string {
build_word_list.go => 	// check word 
build_word_list.go => 	//  return space if not valid
build_word_list.go => 	//  return the same word if OK, sometime with the first character removed 
build_word_list.go => 	//---
build_word_list.go => 	var wor = strings.ToLower(  strings.TrimSpace( word0 ) )
build_word_list.go => 	if ((wor == "") || ( wor == "&amp" )) { 
build_word_list.go => 		return ""
build_word_list.go => 	}
build_word_list.go => 	if wor[0:1] < " "  {
build_word_list.go => 		return ""
build_word_list.go => 	}
build_word_list.go => 	//--------------
build_word_list.go => 	var j1 = strings.IndexAny(wor, "0123456789%|\\_*•-=^&~.,;?!\"'")
build_word_list.go => 	if j1 >=0 {return ""}
build_word_list.go => 	//------------
build_word_list.go => 	var toRemove = "°¿¡€$£"
build_word_list.go => 	var j2 = strings.IndexAny( wor, toRemove)
build_word_list.go => 	if j2 >= 0 {
build_word_list.go => 		// la parola "wor" contiene un carattere da rimuovere
build_word_list.go => 		// se il primo carattere è tra quelli da rimuovere elimino il primo carattere e prendo il resto
build_word_list.go => 		// se non è il primo, allora elimino tutta la parola
build_word_list.go => 		//
build_word_list.go => 		// voglio controllare soltanto il primo carattere,  
build_word_list.go => 		// 		ma un carattere potrebbe essere più lungo di un byte, non posso usare substring  
build_word_list.go => 		
build_word_list.go => 		var wor2 = ""
build_word_list.go => 		for i, letterR := range wor {
build_word_list.go => 			var letter = string(letterR)
build_word_list.go => 			//fmt.Println( "loop rune ", wor,  "  i=", i, " letter=", letter) 
build_word_list.go => 			if (i == 0) { // test primo carattere 
build_word_list.go => 				if strings.IndexAny( string(letter), toRemove) < 0 {
build_word_list.go => 					return ""   // il primo è ok, ma gli altri no 
build_word_list.go => 				} 
build_word_list.go => 				continue
build_word_list.go => 			}
build_word_list.go => 			wor2 += letter 
build_word_list.go => 			//fmt.Println( "loop rune ", wor,  "  i=", i, " letter=", letter,  " wor2=", wor2) 
build_word_list.go => 		}
build_word_list.go => 		wor = wor2
build_word_list.go => 		if strings.IndexAny( wor, toRemove) >= 0 {
build_word_list.go => 			// il carattere strano continua ad esserci, quindi elimino la parola 
build_word_list.go => 			//fmt.Println(" ex loop ",  wor, "    ", "il carattere strano continua ad esserci, quindi elimino la parola " )	
build_word_list.go => 			return ""    
build_word_list.go => 		} 
build_word_list.go => 	} 
build_word_list.go => 	return stdCode(wor)
build_word_list.go => }
build_word_list.go => 
build_word_list.go => //-----------------------------------------
build_word_list.go => 
build_word_list.go => func addCodedWordToWordSlice() {
build_word_list.go => 	/*
build_word_list.go => 	add a sortKeyWord to each word element
build_word_list.go => 	*/
build_word_list.go => 	preW     := "" 	
build_word_list.go => 	preCoded := ""
build_word_list.go => 	//----------------
build_word_list.go => 	for i, wS1 := range wordSliceAlpha {
build_word_list.go => 		if (wS1.wWord2 != preW) { 
build_word_list.go => 			preW = wS1.wWord2	
build_word_list.go => 			preCoded = seqCode(preW)
build_word_list.go => 		}
build_word_list.go => 		wordSliceAlpha[i].wWordSeq = preCoded;
build_word_list.go => 	}	
build_word_list.go => 	//----	
build_word_list.go => 	sort.Slice(wordSliceAlpha, func(i, j int) bool {
build_word_list.go => 		if wordSliceAlpha[i].wWordSeq != wordSliceAlpha[j].wWordSeq {
build_word_list.go => 			return wordSliceAlpha[i].wWordSeq < wordSliceAlpha[j].wWordSeq            // word  ascending order (eg.   a before b ) 
build_word_list.go => 		} else {
build_word_list.go => 			if wordSliceAlpha[i].wWord2 != wordSliceAlpha[j].wWord2 {
build_word_list.go => 				return wordSliceAlpha[i].wWord2 < wordSliceAlpha[j].wWord2  
build_word_list.go => 			} else {
build_word_list.go => 				return wordSliceAlpha[i].wNfile < wordSliceAlpha[j].wNfile          // nFile ascending order (eg.   0 before 1 ) 
build_word_list.go => 			}
build_word_list.go => 		}
build_word_list.go => 	})
build_word_list.go => 	//------------------------------
build_word_list.go => 	
build_word_list.go => 	
build_word_list.go => } // end of addCodedWordToWordSlice
build_word_list.go => 
build_word_list.go => //------------------------------------------------
build_word_list.go => 
build_word_list.go => func addTotRowToWord() {
build_word_list.go => 	/*
build_word_list.go => 	each element of wordSliceAlpha contains a word (the same word may be in several rows) 
build_word_list.go => 	the number of repetition of a word (totRow) is put in its element  ( later will be put in each row that contain it) 
build_word_list.go => 		eg.  one 3, one 3, one 3, two 4, two 4, two 4, two 4	
build_word_list.go => 	*/
build_word_list.go => 	preW  := wordSliceAlpha[0].wWordSeq;	
build_word_list.go => 	totR  := 0	
build_word_list.go => 	ix1   := 0
build_word_list.go => 	ix2   :=-1
build_word_list.go => 	pre_wSwSelRow := SEL_NO_EXTR_ROW 
build_word_list.go => 	//----------------
build_word_list.go => 	tot_extrRow:=0
build_word_list.go => 	for i, wS1 := range wordSliceAlpha {
build_word_list.go => 		
build_word_list.go => 		//fmt.Println("ANTO addTot ... i=" , i , " => ", wS1 )
build_word_list.go => 		
build_word_list.go => 		if (wS1.wWordSeq != preW) {
build_word_list.go => 			ix2 = i; 
build_word_list.go => 			for i2 := ix1; i2 < ix2;i2++ {
build_word_list.go => 				 wordSliceAlpha[i2].wSwSelRowG = pre_wSwSelRow; 	// se esiste almeno un richiamo a una riga estratta ( wSwSelRowR)allora questo segnale è ripetuto come wSwSelRowG
build_word_list.go => 				 wordSliceAlpha[i2].wTotExtrRow = tot_extrRow   
build_word_list.go => 				 wordSliceAlpha[i2].wTotRow    = totR;   // se una parola è ripetuta 3 volte, ad ogni parola è associato 3

color.go =>     var Reset   = "\033[0m" 
color.go => 	var Red     = "\033[31m" 
color.go => 	var Green   = "\033[32m" 
color.go => 	var Yellow  = "\033[33m"

elab_word_list.go => func elabWordAlpha_buildWordFreqList() {
elab_word_list.go => 	/*

elab_word_list.go => 	from wordSliceAlpha list obtain a new list by sorting it by occurrence of the words (totRow) 
elab_word_list.go => 	*/
elab_word_list.go => 	//preW  := ""; // wordSliceAlpha[0].word;

elab_word_list.go => 		if wS1.wWordSeq == LAST_WORD  { wordSliceAlpha[nn].totRow = LAST_WORD_FREQ }
elab_word_list.go => 		//fmt.Println("ANTO elab...FreqList() ", nn, "  alpha=",  wordSliceAlpha[nn]);

elab_word_list.go => 			removeIxWord = -1
elab_word_list.go => 			preW = wS1.wWord2; 
elab_word_list.go => 		}
elab_word_list.go => 		ix =  wS1.ixRow
elab_word_list.go => 		inputTextRowSlice[ix].numWords ++; 		// how many words contains the row (eg. the row "the cat is on the table"  contains 6 words --> .numWords = 6 
elab_word_list.go => 		if removeIxWord >= 0 {
elab_word_list.go => 			//fmt.Println("\t ANTO 2elab" , "  removeIxWord=" , removeIxWord , " wordSliceAlpha[ removeIxWord ].word=" + wordSliceAlpha[ removeIxWord ].word  )
elab_word_list.go => 			
elab_word_list.go => 		    if wS1.wWordSeq == wordSliceAlpha[ removeIxWord ].wWordSeq {
elab_word_list.go => 				wordSliceAlpha[ nn ].sw_ignore = true 
elab_word_list.go => 			}	
elab_word_list.go => 		}	
elab_word_list.go => 		//fmt.Println("\t ANTO XXXelab"  , "  wS1.sw_ignore = ", wS1.sw_ignore)  
elab_word_list.go => 	}
elab_word_list.go => 	*****************************/
elab_word_list.go =>

elab_word_list.go => 	for nn, wS1 := range wordSliceAlpha {	
elab_word_list.go => 		if wS1.wWordSeq == LAST_WORD  { wordSliceAlpha[nn].wTotRow = LAST_WORD_FREQ }	
elab_word_list.go => 		inputTextRowSlice[ wS1.wIxRow ].rNumWords ++; 		// number of words in a row (eg. the row "the cat is on the table"  contains 6 words --> .numWords = 6 
elab_word_list.go => 	}
elab_word_list.go => 	//------------
elab_word_list.go => 	// build WordList by frequence in the text
elab_word_list.go => 	// the slice is sorted in descending order of frequency   ( ie. firstly the most used)   
elab_word_list.go => 	//-----------------------------------
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	wordSliceFreq  = make([]wordStruct, len(wordSliceAlpha),  len(wordSliceAlpha) ) 	 // la slice destinazione del 'copy' deve avere la stessa lunghezza di quella input  
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	copy(wordSliceFreq , wordSliceAlpha);
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	// le parole eguali si trovano in righe contigue perchè hanno la stessa frequenza
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	sort.Slice(wordSliceFreq, func(i, j int) bool {
elab_word_list.go => 			if wordSliceFreq[i].wTotRow !=  wordSliceFreq[j].wTotRow {
elab_word_list.go => 			   return wordSliceFreq[i].wTotRow > wordSliceFreq[j].wTotRow        // totRow    descending order (how many row contain the word) 
elab_word_list.go => 			} else {
elab_word_list.go => 				if wordSliceFreq[i].wWordSeq != wordSliceFreq[j].wWordSeq {
elab_word_list.go => 					return wordSliceFreq[i].wWordSeq < wordSliceFreq[j].wWordSeq            // word  ascending order (eg.   a before b ) 
elab_word_list.go => 				} else {
elab_word_list.go => 					return wordSliceFreq[i].wWord2 < wordSliceFreq[j].wWord2  			
elab_word_list.go => 				}
elab_word_list.go => 			}
elab_word_list.go => 		})
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => } // end of elabWordAlpha_buildWordFreqList

elab_word_list.go => func putWordFrequenceInRowArray() {
elab_word_list.go => 
elab_word_list.go => 	ix:=0;
elab_word_list.go => 
elab_word_list.go => 	//-------------------------	
elab_word_list.go => 	/***
elab_word_list.go => 	//  in each element of inputTextRowSlice define an empty slice to contain the frequence of each of its words
elab_word_list.go => 	for k, _ := range inputTextRowSlice {	
elab_word_list.go => 		tot1 := inputTextRowSlice[k].rNumWords;
elab_word_list.go => 		inputTextRowSlice[k].rListIxUnF =  make( []int, tot1, tot1 )		
elab_word_list.go => 		inputTextRowSlice[k].rListFreq  =  make( []int, tot1, tot1 )			
elab_word_list.go => 	}
elab_word_list.go => 	***/
elab_word_list.go => 	//---------------------------------
elab_word_list.go => 	//  fill each row with the frequence of its words
elab_word_list.go => 	//-------------------	
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	for _, wS1 := range wordSliceFreq {	
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		ix = wS1.wIxRow; 
elab_word_list.go => 		ixPos := wS1.wIxPosRow; 
elab_word_list.go => 		/**
elab_word_list.go => 		fmt.Println("ANTO putWordFrequenceInRowArray() wS1 ", cyan(wS1.wWord2), " wS1.wIxUniq=", wS1.wIxUniq, " wS1.wTotRow=",   wS1.wTotRow ,
elab_word_list.go => 			" ix =", wS1.wIxRow, " ixPos = ", wS1.wIxPosRow); 
elab_word_list.go => 		if ((ix < 0) || (ixPos < 0)) { fmt.Println( red("ERRORE ix, ixPos < 0") ) }
elab_word_list.go => 		**/  
elab_word_list.go => 		/****
elab_word_list.go => 		num2 := len(inputTextRowSlice[ix].rListFreq) 
elab_word_list.go => 		if (num2 <= ixPos) {		
elab_word_list.go => 			fmt.Println("error " , wS1.wWord2, " ix=" , ix  ," ixPos=",   ixPos, " row ", " num2=", num2, " tot1=",  
elab_word_list.go => 				inputTextRowSlice[ix].rNumWords, " list=" , inputTextRowSlice[ix].rListFreq , " " , inputTextRowSlice[ix].rRow1); 			
elab_word_list.go => 		}
elab_word_list.go => 		if ((ixPos<1) || (ix<1)) {
elab_word_list.go => 			fmt.Println( "ERRORe  putWordFrequenceInRowArray() nx=", nx, " WS1=", wS1, "\n\t", "ix=", ix, ", ixPos=", ixPos)  
elab_word_list.go => 		}
elab_word_list.go => 		***/
elab_word_list.go => 		if ixPos < inputTextRowSlice[ix].rNumWords { 
elab_word_list.go => 			inputTextRowSlice[ix].rListIxUnF[ixPos] = wS1.wIxUniq // index of the word in the uniqueWordByFreq  	
elab_word_list.go => 			inputTextRowSlice[ix].rListFreq[ ixPos] = wS1.wTotRow // for each word in the row  set its frequence of use (how many times the word is used in the whole text)  
elab_word_list.go => 		} else {
elab_word_list.go => 			fmt.Println("errore in func ", red( "putWordFrequenceInRowArray"), " row n.", ix, " word pos=", ixPos, " num words in row=",  
elab_word_list.go => 				inputTextRowSlice[ix].rNumWords, " word=", wS1.wWord2, " row=", inputTextRowSlice[ix].rRow1)
elab_word_list.go => 		}	
elab_word_list.go => 	} // end for wS1 range 
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	//---------------------------
elab_word_list.go => } // end of putWordFrequenceInRowArray

elab_word_list.go => func put_a_priority_to_the_row_of_each_word() {
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	//fmt.Println("put_a_priority_to_the_row_of_each_word()")
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	//the row importance is assigned by the number of its unknown words	
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 	for k, wS1 := range wordSliceFreq {	
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		ix := wS1.wIxRow; 
elab_word_list.go => 		numMinor :=0

elab_word_list.go => 
elab_word_list.go => func sortWordListByFreq_and_row_priority() {
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	//fmt.Println(" sortWordListByFreq_and_row_priority()")
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	sort.Slice(wordSliceFreq, func(i, j int) bool {
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		if wordSliceFreq[i].wTotRow !=  wordSliceFreq[j].wTotRow {
elab_word_list.go => 		   return wordSliceFreq[i].wTotRow > wordSliceFreq[j].wTotRow         // totRow    descending order (how many rows contain the word)  	  		   
elab_word_list.go => 		}	
elab_word_list.go => 		if wordSliceFreq[i].wTotExtrRow !=  wordSliceFreq[j].wTotExtrRow {
elab_word_list.go => 		   return wordSliceFreq[i].wTotExtrRow > wordSliceFreq[j].wTotExtrRow  // wTotExtrRow  descending order ( number of extracted rows  if id_sel_2_extrRow option = id="extrRow", else =0) 	
elab_word_list.go => 		}
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		if wordSliceFreq[i].wWordSeq !=  wordSliceFreq[j].wWordSeq {
elab_word_list.go => 		   return wordSliceFreq[i].wWordSeq < wordSliceFreq[j].wWordSeq            // word      ascending order	  		   
elab_word_list.go => 		}	
elab_word_list.go => 		if wordSliceFreq[i].wWord2 !=  wordSliceFreq[j].wWord2 {
elab_word_list.go => 		   return wordSliceFreq[i].wWord2 < wordSliceFreq[j].wWord2                // word      ascending order	  		   
elab_word_list.go => 		}				
elab_word_list.go => 		if wordSliceFreq[i].wTotMinRow !=  wordSliceFreq[j].wTotMinRow {
elab_word_list.go => 		   return wordSliceFreq[i].wTotMinRow < wordSliceFreq[j].wTotMinRow  // totMinRow ascending order	(how many words in the row are not yet learned) 
elab_word_list.go => 		}
elab_word_list.go => 		if wordSliceFreq[i].wSwSelRowR !=  wordSliceFreq[j].wSwSelRowR {
elab_word_list.go => 		   return wordSliceFreq[i].wSwSelRowR < wordSliceFreq[j].wSwSelRowR    // first the extracted row  
elab_word_list.go => 		}
elab_word_list.go => 		return wordSliceFreq[i].wIxRow < wordSliceFreq[j].wIxRow             // ixRow     ascending order	( first the rows which were first in the text)  		   
elab_word_list.go => 			
elab_word_list.go => 	})	
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	/**
elab_word_list.go => 		fmt.Println( "\nANTONIO5 wordSliceFreq -------------------------")
elab_word_list.go => 	for g:=0; g < len( wordSliceFreq ); g++ {
elab_word_list.go => 		fmt.Println( "ANTONIO5 alpha ", wordSliceFreq[g] )
elab_word_list.go => 	}	
elab_word_list.go => 	**/

elab_word_list.go => func compound_lemma( leS lemmaStruct)  string {

elab_word_list.go => 	  if  len(str1) < 4 {return str1} 	  
elab_word_list.go => 	  return str1[4:]
elab_word_list.go => }

elab_word_list.go => func contains(s []int, e int) bool {
elab_word_list.go =>     for _, a := range s {
elab_word_list.go =>         if a == e {
elab_word_list.go =>             return true
elab_word_list.go =>         }
elab_word_list.go =>     }
elab_word_list.go =>     return false
elab_word_list.go => }

elab_word_list.go => func build_listOfLemmaForAWord(uWord string, ixLemmaPairFoundList []int) ([]int, []string) {
elab_word_list.go => 	
elab_word_list.go => 	//sw1:= (strings.Index(uWord, "stellen") >= 0)
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		nele := len(ixLemmaPairFoundList)
elab_word_list.go => 		nele += 20
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		lis_ixLemma1:=make( [] int,    0, nele )				
elab_word_list.go => 		lis_ixLemma2:=make( [] int,    0, nele )		
elab_word_list.go => 		lis_ixLemma3:=make( [] int,    0, nele )
elab_word_list.go => 		lis_origLemma1:= make( []string,    0, nele )	
elab_word_list.go => 		lis_origLemma2:= make( []string,    0, nele )
elab_word_list.go => 		lis_origLemma3:= make( []string,    0, nele )	
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		ixLemma:=-1
elab_word_list.go => 		numLerr:=0; maxNumLerr:=100; 
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		//if uWord == "am" {  fmt.Println(green("0 build_listOfLemmaForAWord") ,  " uWord=", uWord, " ixLemmaPairFoundList " ,ixLemmaPairFoundList) }
elab_word_list.go => 		
elab_word_list.go => 		for  _, ixLp := range ixLemmaPairFoundList { 
elab_word_list.go => 			
elab_word_list.go => 			if numLerr > maxNumLerr { break}
elab_word_list.go => 			if ixLp < 0 {
elab_word_list.go => 				//lemma3 := "?" + uWord	
elab_word_list.go => 				ixLemma = -1; // addUnknowToLemma(lemma3) 
elab_word_list.go => 			} else {
elab_word_list.go => 				newWL := wordLemmaPair[ixLp]
elab_word_list.go => 				if newWL.lWord2 != uWord { // error 
elab_word_list.go => 					continue; 
elab_word_list.go => 				}

elab_word_list.go => 		listExam:= ""

elab_word_list.go => 				}
elab_word_list.go => 			}
elab_word_list.go => 			listPara += "|" + parad  		 	
elab_word_list.go => 			listExam += "|" + lemma_para_list[ix].p_example 	
elab_word_list.go => 	 		//fmt.Println("\t ", ix, " listLev =", listLev)    
elab_word_list.go => 		}

elab_word_list.go => 			listLev = listLev[1:]

encode_word.go => package wbfSubPack
encode_word.go => 
encode_word.go => import (  
encode_word.go => 	//"fmt"
encode_word.go =>     "strings"
encode_word.go => )
encode_word.go => 
encode_word.go => //=================================================
encode_word.go => /**
encode_word.go => var translate_chars_std_inp = "" ; // "ä,ö,ü,ß";     
encode_word.go => var translate_chars_std_out = "" ; // "ae,oe,ue,ss"
encode_word.go => //--   
encode_word.go => var translate_chars_SEQ_inp = "ä,ö,ü,ß";      
encode_word.go => var translate_chars_SEQ_out = "aä,oö,uü,ssß"
encode_word.go => **/
encode_word.go => // 	 can be used in case a character might be written in more than one way:   eg. ß = ss        
encode_word.go => var translate_chars_std_inpList []string	 // filled by inputLanguage.txt file in func read_languageFile     
encode_word.go => var translate_chars_std_outList []string	 // filled by inputLanguage.txt file in func read_languageFile       
encode_word.go => //--   
encode_word.go => //   to modify the the place of a character in the alphabetic sequence order: eg.  'aä' replaces 'ä' so that the 'ä' is immediately after the normal 'a' 
encode_word.go => //					                eg. german ==>  inp = "ä,ö,ü,ß"  / out "aä,oö,uü,ssß"
encode_word.go => 
encode_word.go => var translate_chars_SEQ_inpList []string     // filled by inputLanguage.txt file in func read_languageFile           
encode_word.go => var translate_chars_SEQ_outList []string	 // filled by inputLanguage.txt file in func read_languageFile     
encode_word.go => 
encode_word.go => //=================================================
encode_word.go => 
encode_word.go => func stdCode(inpCode string ) string {		
encode_word.go => 	
encode_word.go => 	if len(translate_chars_std_inpList) == 0 {return inpCode}
encode_word.go => 	
encode_word.go => 	outCode:= inpCode 
encode_word.go => 	for nn,ch1:= range translate_chars_std_inpList {
encode_word.go => 		outCode = strings.ReplaceAll(outCode, ch1, translate_chars_std_outList[nn])  
encode_word.go => 	}		
encode_word.go => 	
encode_word.go => 	return outCode  
encode_word.go => 
encode_word.go => } // end of stdCode
encode_word.go => 
encode_word.go => //----------------------------
encode_word.go => func seqCode( inpCode string ) string {	
encode_word.go => 	/*
encode_word.go => 	serve soprattutto per mettere le parole in sequenza alfabetica più naturale di quella dettata dal codifica asci o utf8 
encode_word.go => 	es. per il tedesco es.  ä, ö, ü, ß vicini rispettivamente ad a, o, u, ss)   	
encode_word.go => 	*/	
encode_word.go => 	
encode_word.go => 	if len(translate_chars_SEQ_inpList) == 0 {return inpCode}
encode_word.go => 	
encode_word.go => 	outCode:= inpCode 
encode_word.go => 	for nn,ch1:= range translate_chars_SEQ_inpList {
encode_word.go => 		outCode = strings.ReplaceAll(outCode, ch1, translate_chars_SEQ_outList[nn])  
encode_word.go => 	}		
encode_word.go => 	return outCode  
encode_word.go => 	
encode_word.go => }// end of seqCode

17 __FILE: go_exec_js_function.go
go_exec_js_function.go => package wbfSubPack
go_exec_js_function.go => 
go_exec_js_function.go => import (  
go_exec_js_function.go => 	"fmt"
go_exec_js_function.go =>     "strings"
go_exec_js_function.go => 	"runtime"
go_exec_js_function.go => )
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => //----------------------------------------------------------------
go_exec_js_function.go => func go_exec_js_function(js_function0 string, inpstr string) {
go_exec_js_function.go => 	var goFunc string 
go_exec_js_function.go =>  	pc, _, _, ok := runtime.Caller(1)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	details := runtime.FuncForPC(pc)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	if ok && details != nil {
go_exec_js_function.go => 		goFunc = strings.ReplaceAll(details.Name(), "main.","")
go_exec_js_function.go => 	} else {
go_exec_js_function.go => 		goFunc=""
go_exec_js_function.go => 	}
go_exec_js_function.go => 	js_fun        := strings.Split( (js_function0 + ",,,,") ,",") 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	js_function   := strings.TrimSpace( js_fun[0] )
go_exec_js_function.go => 	if js_function == ""  { return }
go_exec_js_function.go => 	jsInpFunction := strings.TrimSpace( js_fun[1] )
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	js_parm:=""
go_exec_js_function.go => 	k1:= strings.Index(js_function, "(") 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	if k1 > 0 {
go_exec_js_function.go => 		js_parm     = strings.ReplaceAll(  js_function[k1+1:], ")","")			
go_exec_js_function.go => 		js_function = strings.TrimSpace(js_function[0:k1] )
go_exec_js_function.go => 	}	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	/**
go_exec_js_function.go => 	fmt.Println("  js_function=" + js_function)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	fmt.Println("      js_parm=" + js_parm)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	fmt.Println("jsInpFunction=" + jsInpFunction)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	fmt.Println("       goFunc=" + goFunc)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	**/
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	/*
go_exec_js_function.go => 	This function executes a javascript eval command 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	which must execute a function by passing string constant to it. 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	Should this string contain some new line, e syntax error would occur in eval the statement.
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	To avoid this kind of error, the string argument (inpstr) of the javascript function (js_function) 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	is forced to be always enclosed in back ticks trasforming it in "template literal".  
go_exec_js_function.go => 	Just in case back ticks and dollars are in the string, they are replaced by " "   	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	*/
go_exec_js_function.go => 	inpstr = strings.ReplaceAll( inpstr, "`", " "   ); 	   	 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	inpstr = strings.ReplaceAll( inpstr, "$", "&dollar;"); 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	//fmt.Println(cyan("go_exec_js_function "), "js_function=", js_function, "js_parm=", js_parm, " jsInpFunction=", jsInpFunction, " gofunc=", "go=" + goFunc)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	//fmt.Println("go_exec_js_function ", "	inpstr=", inpstr)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	evalStr := fmt.Sprintf( "%s(`%s`,`%s`,`%s`,`%s`);",  js_function, inpstr, js_parm, "js=" + jsInpFunction, "go=" + goFunc ) ; 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	//fmt.Println(cyan("go_exec_js_function "), "evalStr=" + evalStr); 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	ui.Eval(evalStr)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => } // end of go_exec_js_function
go_exec_js_function.go => //----------------------------------------------------------------
go_exec_js_function.go => 
go_exec_js_function.go => func go_exec_js_functionX(js_function string, inpstr string ) {
go_exec_js_function.go => 	/*
go_exec_js_function.go => 	This function executes a javascript eval command 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	which must execute a function by passing string constant to it. 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	Should this string contain some new line, e syntax error would occur in eval the statement.
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	To avoid this kind of error, the string argument (inpstr) of the javascript function (js_function) 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	is forced to be always enclosed in back ticks trasforming it in "template literal".  
go_exec_js_function.go => 	Just in case back ticks and dollars are in the string, they are replaced by their html symbols.   	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	*/
go_exec_js_function.go => 	inpstr = strings.ReplaceAll( inpstr, "`", " "   ); 	  
go_exec_js_function.go => 	//inpstr = strings.ReplaceAll( inpstr, "`", "&#96;"   ); 	 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	inpstr = strings.ReplaceAll( inpstr, "$", "&dollar;"); 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	evalStr := fmt.Sprintf( "%s(`%s`);",  js_function,  inpstr ) ; 
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => 	ui.Eval(evalStr)
go_exec_js_function.go => 	
go_exec_js_function.go => } // end of go_exec_js_function
go_exec_js_function.go => 
go_exec_js_function.go => //--------------------------------

html_env.go => func getScreenXY() (int, int) {
html_env.go => 	
html_env.go => 	// use ==>  var x, y int = getScreenXY();
html_env.go => 	
html_env.go => 	var width  int = int(win.GetSystemMetrics(win.SM_CXSCREEN));
html_env.go => 	var height int = int(win.GetSystemMetrics(win.SM_CYSCREEN));
html_env.go => 	if width == 0 || height == 0 {
html_env.go => 		//fmt.Println( "errore" )
html_env.go => 		return 2000, 2000; 
html_env.go => 	}	
html_env.go => 	width  = width  - 20;  // subtraction to make room for any decorations 
html_env.go => 	height = height - 40;  // subtraction to make room for any decorations 
html_env.go => 	
html_env.go => 	return width, height
html_env.go => }

html_env.go => func getCompleteHtmlPath( path_html string) string {
html_env.go => 	
html_env.go => 	//curDir    := "D:/ANTONIO/K_L_M_N/LINGUAGGI/GO/_WORDS_BY_FREQUENCE/WbF_prova1_input_piccolo
html_env.go => 	 
html_env.go => 	curDir, err := os.Getwd()
html_env.go =>     if err != nil {
html_env.go => 		fmt.Println("setHtmlEnv() 3 err=", err )
html_env.go =>         //log.Fatal(err)
html_env.go =>     }	
html_env.go => 				
html_env.go => 	fmt.Println("curDir           = " + curDir ); 
html_env.go => 	
html_env.go => 	curDirBack  := curDir
html_env.go => 	k1:= strings.LastIndex(curDir, "/") 
html_env.go => 	k2:= strings.LastIndex(curDir, "\\") 
html_env.go => 	if k2 > k1 { k1 = k2 } 
html_env.go => 	curDirBack = curDir[0:k1] 	
html_env.go => 	
html_env.go => 	var newPath string = ""
html_env.go => 	if strings.Index(path_html,":") > 0 {
html_env.go => 		newPath = path_html
html_env.go => 	} else if path_html[0:2] == ".." {
html_env.go => 		newPath = curDirBack  + path_html[2:] 
html_env.go => 	} else {
html_env.go => 		newPath = curDir + path_html
html_env.go => 	}
html_env.go => 	return newPath 
html_env.go => }

html_env.go => 	err1:= `document.getElementById("id_startwait").innerHTML = '<br><br> <span style="color:red;">§msg1§</span> <span style="color:blue;">§inpFile§</span>';` ; 		
html_env.go => 	err1 = strings.ReplaceAll( err1, "§msg1§", msg1 ); 	 
html_env.go => 	err1 = strings.ReplaceAll( err1, "§inpFile§", inpFile); 	
html_env.go => 	ui.Eval( err1 );	
html_env.go => }

lookFor.go => func lookForLemma(lemmaTarg string) (int, int) {
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	// find 2 indices of the 2 words nearest to the word to find 
lookFor.go => 	/**
lookFor.go => 				
lookFor.go => 			//---
lookFor.go => 			type lemmaStruct struct {
lookFor.go => 				leLemma     string
lookFor.go => 				leNumWords  int 
lookFor.go => 				leFromIxLW  int 
lookFor.go => 				leToIxLW    int  
lookFor.go => 				leTran      string 
lookFor.go => 				leLevel     string  
lookFor.go => 				lePara      string  
lookFor.go => 				leExample   string  	
lookFor.go => 			} 	
lookFor.go => 			var lemmaSlice       [] lemmaStruct         // lemma , translation 
lookFor.go => 	***/
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	low   := 0
lookFor.go => 	high  := len(lemmaSlice) - 1	
lookFor.go => 	maxIx := high; 
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	//----
lookFor.go => 	for low <= high{
lookFor.go => 		median := (low + high) / 2
lookFor.go => 		if lemmaSlice[median].leLemma < lemmaTarg {  
lookFor.go => 			low = median + 1
lookFor.go => 		}else{
lookFor.go => 			high = median - 1
lookFor.go => 		}
lookFor.go => 	} 
lookFor.go => 	//---
lookFor.go => 	fromIx:= low; toIx := high; 
lookFor.go => 	if fromIx > toIx { fromIx = high; toIx = low;}
lookFor.go => 	if fromIx < 0 { fromIx=0} 
lookFor.go => 	if toIx  > maxIx { toIx = maxIx}
lookFor.go => 	return fromIx, toIx	
lookFor.go => 
lookFor.go => } // end of LookForLemma

lookFor.go => 			maxIx = k; 
lookFor.go => 			if minIx < 0 { minIx = k}

lookFor.go => 			if lemma_para_list[k].p_lemma > lemma3 { break }
lookFor.go => 		}
lookFor.go => 	}

lookFor.go => func searchAllWordWithPrefixInAlphaList(  wordPref string) (int, int) {
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	// get the indicies of the first and the last word beginning with the required prefix (-1,-1 if not found)  
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	wordPref = strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace( wordPref));  
lookFor.go => 	wordCodPref:= seqCode(wordPref)
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	lenPref:= len(wordPref); 
lookFor.go => 	ixTo := -1; ixFrom:= -1;	
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	if lenPref == 0 { return ixFrom, ixTo }
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	ix1, ix2:= lookForWordInUniqueAlpha(wordCodPref)	
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	/***
lookFor.go => 	fmt.Println("ANTONIO SEARCH ALPHA wordPref=" + wordPref + " wordCodPref=" +  wordCodPref + " ix1=", ix1, " ix2=", ix2) 
lookFor.go => 	for k:= ix1; k <= ix2; k++ {
lookFor.go => 		if k < 0 { continue}
lookFor.go => 		fmt.Println("ANTONIO SEARCH ALPHA k=", k , " ==>" , uniqueWordByAlpha[k])
lookFor.go => 	}	
lookFor.go => 	***/
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	wA :=""
lookFor.go => 	spaceFill := "                                                          ";  
lookFor.go => 	//-----------
lookFor.go => 	for k:= ix1; k >= 0; k-- {
lookFor.go => 		wA =  uniqueWordByAlpha[k].uWordSeq + spaceFill
lookFor.go => 		if wA[0:lenPref] < wordCodPref { break; }
lookFor.go => 		ixFrom = k; 
lookFor.go => 	}  
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	if (ixFrom >=0) { ixTo = ixFrom; }  //  se ixFrom è valido, deve essere valido anche ixTo   
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => 	for k:= ix2; k < numberOfUniqueWords; k++ {
lookFor.go => 		wA =  uniqueWordByAlpha[k].uWordSeq + spaceFill  
lookFor.go => 		if wA[0:lenPref] > wordCodPref { break; }
lookFor.go => 		ixTo = k; 
lookFor.go => 		if (ixFrom < 0) {ixFrom = ixTo;}  //  se ixTo è valido, deve essere valido anche ixFrom   
lookFor.go => 	}  
lookFor.go => 	return ixFrom, ixTo 
lookFor.go => 	
lookFor.go => } // end of searchAllWordWithPrefixInAlphaList

myMain.go => func MyMain() {
myMain.go =>

myMain.go => 	fmt.Println(  red("WordsByFrequence - wbfMain") )

read_any_text_file.go => func rowListFromFile( path1 string, fileName string, descr string, func1 string, bytesPerRow int ) []string {

read_any_text_file.go => 		return nil
read_any_text_file.go => 	}

read_any_text_file.go =>

read_any_text_file.go => 	
read_any_text_file.go => 	retRowL := make( [] string, 0, numEleMax)	
read_any_text_file.go => 	
read_any_text_file.go => 	r := bufio.NewReader(file)
read_any_text_file.go => 	for {
read_any_text_file.go => 	  line, _, err := r.ReadLine()
read_any_text_file.go => 	  if err != nil {
read_any_text_file.go => 		if err == io.EOF {
read_any_text_file.go => 			break
read_any_text_file.go => 		}
read_any_text_file.go => 		break
read_any_text_file.go => 	  }	 
read_any_text_file.go => 	  retRowL = append( retRowL, string(line) ) 	
read_any_text_file.go => 	}
read_any_text_file.go => 	defer file.Close()
read_any_text_file.go => 	
read_any_text_file.go => 	fmt.Println("letto file " , fileName, "  num lines=", len(retRowL) )
read_any_text_file.go => 	
read_any_text_file.go => 	return retRowL 
read_any_text_file.go => 	
read_any_text_file.go => } // end of rowListFromFile

read_paradigma.go => 		} else {
read_paradigma.go => 			if lemma_para_list[i].p_level != lemma_para_list[j].p_level {
read_paradigma.go => 				return lemma_para_list[i].p_level < lemma_para_list[j].p_level     // level ascending order (eg.   A1 before A2 ) 
read_paradigma.go => 			} else {
read_paradigma.go => 				return lemma_para_list[i].p_para < lemma_para_list[j].p_para       // level ascending order (eg.   a  before b ) 
read_paradigma.go => 			}
read_paradigma.go => 		}	
read_paradigma.go => 	} )	
read_paradigma.go => 
read_paradigma.go => } // end of sort_lemmaPara()

read_translation.go => func sort_lemmaTran2() {
read_translation.go => 	
read_translation.go => 	if len(dictLemmaTran) < 1 { return }
read_translation.go => 	
read_translation.go => 	sort.Slice(dictLemmaTran, func(i, j int) bool {
read_translation.go => 			if (dictLemmaTran[i].dL_lemmaSeq != dictLemmaTran[j].dL_lemmaSeq) { 
read_translation.go => 				return dictLemmaTran[i].dL_lemmaSeq < dictLemmaTran[j].dL_lemmaSeq 
read_translation.go => 			} else {
read_translation.go => 				return dictLemmaTran[i].dL_numDict < dictLemmaTran[j].dL_numDict				
read_translation.go => 			}
read_translation.go => 		} )		
read_translation.go => 	//------------	
read_translation.go => 	var pre lemmaTranStruct
read_translation.go => 	pre = dictLemmaTran[0]
read_translation.go => 	//nelle doppie mette codice X'ff'  valore massio di un byte
read_translation.go => 	for g2:=1; g2 < len(dictLemmaTran); g2++ {
read_translation.go => 		if (dictLemmaTran[g2].dL_lemmaSeq == pre.dL_lemmaSeq) {
read_translation.go => 			dictLemmaTran[g2 -1].dL_lemmaSeq = "";  //LAST_WORD; 
read_translation.go => 		} 
read_translation.go => 		pre = dictLemmaTran[g2] 
read_translation.go => 	}
read_translation.go => 	//--------------------------
read_translation.go => 	// sort in modo da sbattere i codice XX'ff ( cioè le doppie) alla fine 
read_translation.go => 	sort.Slice(dictLemmaTran, func(i, j int) bool {
read_translation.go => 			if (dictLemmaTran[i].dL_lemmaSeq != dictLemmaTran[j].dL_lemmaSeq) { 
read_translation.go => 				return dictLemmaTran[i].dL_lemmaSeq < dictLemmaTran[j].dL_lemmaSeq 
read_translation.go => 			} else {
read_translation.go => 				return dictLemmaTran[i].dL_numDict < dictLemmaTran[j].dL_numDict				
read_translation.go => 			}
read_translation.go => 		} )		
read_translation.go => 	//--------------------------------	
read_translation.go => 	var numLin = 0
read_translation.go => 	// cerca dove si trova il primo codice X'ff' per trovare la lunghezza effettiva dell'array
read_translation.go => 	
read_translation.go => 	firstIx:=-1
read_translation.go => 	
read_translation.go => 	for g2:=0; g2 < len(dictLemmaTran); g2++ {
read_translation.go => 		
read_translation.go => 		//if strings.Index(dictLemmaTran[g2].dL_lemmaSeq, "eindhoven") >= 0 { fmt.Println( "in sort_lemaa_tran2 ", " g2=", g2, " ",  dictLemmaTran[g2] ) }
read_translation.go => 		
read_translation.go => 		if (dictLemmaTran[g2].dL_lemmaSeq == "") {
read_translation.go => 			firstIx = g2 	
read_translation.go => 		} else {
read_translation.go => 			numLin++
read_translation.go => 		}
read_translation.go => 	}
read_translation.go => 	//fmt.Println("sort_lemmaTran2 ", " last blank=", firstIx, " len=",  len(dictLemmaTran)); 
read_translation.go => 	firstIx++
read_translation.go => 	if numLin > 0 {
read_translation.go => 		dictLemmaTran = dictLemmaTran[firstIx:]
read_translation.go => 	}
read_translation.go => 	
read_translation.go => 	
read_translation.go => } // end of sort_lemmaTran2()

read_word_lemma.go => 			wordLemma1.lLemma   = stdCode( strings.TrimSpace( cols[1] )	)

read_word_lemma.go => func addToCurrentLemmaPair(wordLemmaPairTMP []wordLemmaPairStruct ) {
read_word_lemma.go => 	sort.Strings(lemmaNotFoundList)
read_word_lemma.go => 	preL := ""
read_word_lemma.go => 	for _, oneLemma:= range lemmaNotFoundList { 
read_word_lemma.go => 		if oneLemma == preL { continue } 
read_word_lemma.go => 		preL = oneLemma  
read_word_lemma.go => 		var wordLemma1 wordLemmaPairStruct 
read_word_lemma.go => 		wordLemma1.lWord2   = stdCode( oneLemma ) 		
read_word_lemma.go => 		wordLemma1.lLemma   = wordLemma1.lWord2 
read_word_lemma.go => 		if len(wordLemma1.lLemma) < 1 { continue;  } 
read_word_lemma.go => 		if ((wordLemma1.lLemma == "-") || (wordLemma1.lLemma[0:1] < "A")) { continue;  }   // ignore number  
read_word_lemma.go => 		wordLemma1.lWordSeq = seqCode( wordLemma1.lWord2)
read_word_lemma.go => 		wordLemma1.lIxLemma = -1	
read_word_lemma.go => 		wordLemmaPairTMP = append(wordLemmaPairTMP, wordLemma1 ) 
read_word_lemma.go => 	}

separablePrefixes.go => 		}
separablePrefixes.go => 	}

separablePrefixes.go => func get_separablePrefix( stringPrefissiSeparabili string ) {
separablePrefixes.go => 	//----------------------------------------
separablePrefixes.go => 	/***
separablePrefixes.go => 	//  "ab, an, auf, aus, bei, ein, fern,   her, herein, hin, hinaus, los, mit, nach, vor, vorbei, weg, weiter, zu, zurück, zusammen, zuruck"  
separablePrefixes.go => 	var stringPrefissiSeparabili = ""+ 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"ab - da;" + 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"an  - a, addosso       ;"	+ 	
separablePrefixes.go => 		"auf - su, verso l'alto ;"  +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"aus - fuori, all'infuori;" + 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"bei   - a;" +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"ein - dentro, verso l'interno;" + 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"fern - lontano;" +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"her - avanti;"+
separablePrefixes.go => 		"herein - dentro;" +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"hin - a;" +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"hinaus - fuori;" +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"los - andare;" + 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"mit - con;" +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"nach - a;" + 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"vor  - davanti, prima;" +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"vorbei- passato;" + 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"weg - lontano;" +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"weiter - più lontano, continuare;"   +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"zu     - chiuso, verso il basso;" +
separablePrefixes.go => 		"zurück - indietro, ritorno;"      + 		 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"zusammen - insieme;" + 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"zuruck - indietro;" + 
separablePrefixes.go => 		"";
separablePrefixes.go => 	****/	
separablePrefixes.go => 		
separablePrefixes.go => 	//----------------------------------------
separablePrefixes.go => 	
separablePrefixes.go => 	separPrefList = make([]separPrefStruct, 0, 200) 
separablePrefixes.go => 	var sP separPrefStruct 
separablePrefixes.go => 	listPr:= strings.Split(  strings.ToLower(stringPrefissiSeparabili),  ";" ) 	
separablePrefixes.go => 	for _,lP:= range listPr {
separablePrefixes.go => 		if lP == "" {continue}
separablePrefixes.go => 		lP+= " - - - "
separablePrefixes.go => 		pp:= strings.Split(lP,"-")
separablePrefixes.go => 		
separablePrefixes.go => 	    sP.sPrefix  = strings.TrimSpace( pp[0] ) 
separablePrefixes.go => 		sP.sPrefTran= strings.TrimSpace( pp[1] ) 
separablePrefixes.go => 		sP.sLenPref = len(sP.sPrefix) 
separablePrefixes.go => 		separPrefList = append(separPrefList, sP) 
separablePrefixes.go => 	}	
separablePrefixes.go => 	//----- sort firstly the longest prefix, then ascending prefix order   
separablePrefixes.go => 	sort.Slice(separPrefList, func(i, j int) bool { 
separablePrefixes.go => 			if separPrefList[i].sLenPref != separPrefList[j].sLenPref {
separablePrefixes.go => 				return separPrefList[i].sLenPref > separPrefList[j].sLenPref
separablePrefixes.go => 			} else {			
separablePrefixes.go => 				return separPrefList[i].sPrefix < separPrefList[j].sPrefix 		 	
separablePrefixes.go => 			} 
separablePrefixes.go => 		})	 	
separablePrefixes.go => 	
separablePrefixes.go => 	fmt.Println("\n\nXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX")
separablePrefixes.go => 	for _, p:= range separPrefList {
separablePrefixes.go => 		fmt.Println("separable prefix ", p.sPrefix , " \t ", p.sPrefTran)
separablePrefixes.go => 	}
separablePrefixes.go => 	fmt.Println("XXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX\n")
separablePrefixes.go => 	
separablePrefixes.go => }
separablePrefixes.go => //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------

statistics.go => func prtFloat( input float64, maxL int, dec1 int ) string {
statistics.go => 	//  space character after % is the padding character which will be repeated by the value replacing the first *   
statistics.go =>     //  first  * character is replaced by the difference between the maximum length and the actual length of the number converted to string 
statistics.go =>     //  second * character is replaced by dec1 value ( how many decimal)    	
statistics.go => 	return fmt.Sprintf("% *s%.*f", maxL-len( strconv.FormatFloat(input, 'f', 2, 64)), "", dec1,  input )
statistics.go => } 
statistics.go => //--------------------------
statistics.go => func prtFloat1( input float64, maxL int ) string {
statistics.go =>   return fmt.Sprintf("% *s%.1f", maxL-len( strconv.FormatFloat(input, 'f', 2, 64)), "",  input )
statistics.go => } 
statistics.go => //---
statistics.go => func prtInt( input int, maxL int ) string {
statistics.go =>   return fmt.Sprintf("% *s%d", maxL-len( strconv.Itoa(input)), "",  input )
statistics.go => }

suffix_lemma_word.go => type inverseStruct  struct {

suffix_lemma_word.go => 	inIx      int 
suffix_lemma_word.go => }
suffix_lemma_word.go => //-----------------------------------------------------------
suffix_lemma_word.go => func reverseString(s string) string {
suffix_lemma_word.go =>     runes := []rune(s)
suffix_lemma_word.go =>     length := len(runes)
suffix_lemma_word.go => 
suffix_lemma_word.go =>     for i := 0; i < length/2; i++ {
suffix_lemma_word.go =>         // Swap characters from the start and end
suffix_lemma_word.go =>         runes[i], runes[length-1-i] = runes[length-1-i], runes[i]
suffix_lemma_word.go =>     }
suffix_lemma_word.go => 
suffix_lemma_word.go =>     return string(runes)
suffix_lemma_word.go => }
suffix_lemma_word.go => 
suffix_lemma_word.go => //-----------------------------------------------------------
suffix_lemma_word.go => 
suffix_lemma_word.go => func lookForInverse(invTarg string, inverseSlice []inverseStruct) (int, int) {
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	// find 2 indices of the 2 words nearest to the word to find 
suffix_lemma_word.go => 
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	low   := 0
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	high  := len(inverseSlice) - 1	
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	maxIx := high; 
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	//----
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	for low <= high{
suffix_lemma_word.go => 		median := (low + high) / 2
suffix_lemma_word.go => 		if inverseSlice[median].inInverse < invTarg {  
suffix_lemma_word.go => 			low = median + 1
suffix_lemma_word.go => 		}else{
suffix_lemma_word.go => 			high = median - 1
suffix_lemma_word.go => 		}
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	} 
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	//---
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	fromIx:= low; toIx := high; 
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	if fromIx > toIx { fromIx = high; toIx = low;}
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	if fromIx < 0 { fromIx=0} 
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	if toIx  > maxIx { toIx = maxIx}
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	return fromIx, toIx	
suffix_lemma_word.go => 
suffix_lemma_word.go => } // end of lookForInverse
suffix_lemma_word.go => 
suffix_lemma_word.go => //=============================================================================

suffix_lemma_word.go => 	
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	for ix1, dirLem := range lemmaSlice {
suffix_lemma_word.go => 		oneInv.inInverse = reverseString( dirLem.leLemma ) 
suffix_lemma_word.go => 		oneInv.inIx      = ix1		
suffix_lemma_word.go => 		inverseLemmaSlice = append( inverseLemmaSlice, oneInv )
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	}  
suffix_lemma_word.go => 		
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	sort.Slice(inverseLemmaSlice, func(i, j int) bool {
suffix_lemma_word.go => 				return inverseLemmaSlice[i].inInverse < inverseLemmaSlice[j].inInverse
suffix_lemma_word.go => 			}   )		
suffix_lemma_word.go => 	fmt.Println( green("loadInverseLemmaSlice") , " caricati ", len(inverseLemmaSlice) , " lemma inversi")

write_and_rewrite.go => func rewrite_word_lemma_dictionary() {	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	//----------------------------------------------------
write_and_rewrite.go => 	sort.Slice(newWordLemmaPair, func(i, j int) bool {
write_and_rewrite.go => 			if (newWordLemmaPair[i].lWordSeq != newWordLemmaPair[j].lWordSeq) {
write_and_rewrite.go => 				return newWordLemmaPair[i].lWordSeq < newWordLemmaPair[j].lWordSeq
write_and_rewrite.go => 			} else {
write_and_rewrite.go => 				if (newWordLemmaPair[i].lWord2 != newWordLemmaPair[j].lWord2) {
write_and_rewrite.go => 					return newWordLemmaPair[i].lWord2 < newWordLemmaPair[j].lWord2 
write_and_rewrite.go => 				} else {
write_and_rewrite.go => 					return newWordLemmaPair[i].lLemma < newWordLemmaPair[j].lLemma
write_and_rewrite.go => 				}
write_and_rewrite.go => 			}
write_and_rewrite.go => 		} )		 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	//------------			
write_and_rewrite.go => 	outFile := FOLDER_OUTPUT +  string(os.PathSeparator) + FILE_outWordLemmaDict ;		
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	lines:= make([]string, 0, 10+len(newWordLemmaPair) )
write_and_rewrite.go => 
write_and_rewrite.go => 	lines = append(lines,  "__" + outFile + "\n" + "_word _lemma ")
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go =>  	for z:=0; z < len( newWordLemmaPair); z++ {
write_and_rewrite.go => 		//lines = append(lines,  newWordLemmaPair[z].lWord2 + "|" + newWordLemmaPair[z].lLemma) 
write_and_rewrite.go => 		lines = append(lines,  newWordLemmaPair[z].lWord2 + " " + newWordLemmaPair[z].lLemma) 
write_and_rewrite.go => 	}  	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go =>     writeList( outFile, lines )
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => } // end of rewrite_word_lemma_dictionary
write_and_rewrite.go => 
write_and_rewrite.go => //--------------------------------

write_and_rewrite.go => func rewrite_LemmaTranDict_file() {
write_and_rewrite.go => 
write_and_rewrite.go => 	//fmt.Println( "GO ", green("rewrite_LemmaTranDict_file" ))
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	//outFile := FOLDER_IO_lastTRAN +  string(os.PathSeparator) + FILE_ outLemmaTranDict;
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	outFile := FOLDER_IO_lastTRAN  +  string(os.PathSeparator) + FILE_last_updated_dict_words 
write_and_rewrite.go => 
write_and_rewrite.go => 	pkey := ""; key := ""
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	lines:= make([]string, 0, 10+len(dictLemmaTran) )
write_and_rewrite.go => 	lines = append(lines,  "__" + outFile + "\n" + "_lemma	_traduzione")
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	for z:=0; z < len(dictLemmaTran); z++ {
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 		pkey=key
write_and_rewrite.go => 		
write_and_rewrite.go => 		
write_and_rewrite.go => 		
write_and_rewrite.go => 		key = dictLemmaTran[z].dL_lemma2 + "|"  + dictLemmaTran[z].dL_tran  		////cigna1_3
write_and_rewrite.go => 		
write_and_rewrite.go => 		//if strings.Index(key,"eindhoven")>=0 { fmt.Println("rewrite_LemmaTranDict_file ",  dictLemmaTran[z],  " key=",key, " pkey=", pkey) }
write_and_rewrite.go => 		
write_and_rewrite.go => 		if pkey == key { continue}
write_and_rewrite.go => 		
write_and_rewrite.go => 		lines = append(lines, key ) 
write_and_rewrite.go => 	}
write_and_rewrite.go => 	writeList( outFile, lines )
write_and_rewrite.go => 	//--------------------
write_and_rewrite.go => 
write_and_rewrite.go => 	currentTime := time.Now()		
write_and_rewrite.go => 	outF1 		:= FOLDER_O_arc_TRAN_words +  string(os.PathSeparator) + "dictL"  		
write_and_rewrite.go => 	outFile2 := outF1 + currentTime.Format("20060102150405") + ".txt"
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	writeList( outFile2, lines )
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => 	
write_and_rewrite.go => } // end of rewrite_LemmaTranDict_file
write_and_rewrite.go => 
write_and_rewrite.go => //----------------------
write_and_rewrite.go => func writeList( fileName string, lines []string)  {
write_and_rewrite.go => 	// create file
write_and_rewrite.go =>     f, err := os.Create( fileName )
write_and_rewrite.go =>     if err != nil {
write_and_rewrite.go =>         fmt.Println( red("error")," in writeList file=", fileName,"\n\t" , err ) //  log.Fatal(err)
write_and_rewrite.go =>     }
write_and_rewrite.go =>     // remember to close the file
write_and_rewrite.go =>     defer f.Close()
write_and_rewrite.go => 
write_and_rewrite.go =>     // create new buffer
write_and_rewrite.go =>     buffer := bufio.NewWriter(f)
write_and_rewrite.go => 
write_and_rewrite.go =>     for _, line := range lines {
write_and_rewrite.go =>         _, err := buffer.WriteString(line + "\n")
write_and_rewrite.go =>         if err != nil {
write_and_rewrite.go =>            fmt.Println( red("error"), " in buffer.WriteString file=", fileName,"\n\t" , err ) //log.Fatal(err)
write_and_rewrite.go =>         }
write_and_rewrite.go =>     }
write_and_rewrite.go =>     // flush buffered data to the file
write_and_rewrite.go =>     if err := buffer.Flush(); err != nil {
write_and_rewrite.go =>         fmt.Println( red("error"), " in buffer.Flush()cls file=", fileName,"\n\t" , err ) //  log.Fatal(err)
write_and_rewrite.go =>     }
write_and_rewrite.go => } 
write_and_rewrite.go => //----------------------------------------
